51-### C. New findings
52-
53:**N56 (moderate; sharpens 8, 11, 15, 24 and N54). Without price events Replay can never trade, so Production's signal-path hard exits never run in Replay.**
54-
55-- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** How it works today:
56:  - `exactPriceReplay = !livePrices.isEmpty()` is set once per run (`ShadowProductionReplayService.java:95`). It isn't just a label: the signal-close protection block runs only when it is false (`:183`).
57:  - Opening or adding to a position needs a fresh execution price (`:310-312`, `:345-347`). In Replay that price comes only from `replayScope.latestPrice()` (`ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java:37-43`).
58:  - `latestPrice` is set only by `referencePrice(...)` (`ExecutionReplayScope.java:73-77`), and its only caller is the price-event loop (`:130`). Every run starts with it null (`ExecutionReplayScope.java:37-45`).
59-- **[AGENT INFERENCE, deduced from the code above]** Consequences:
60:  - A window with no accepted price events can never open a position. Every `SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK` run reports zero trades.
61:  - An open position means at least one event was consumed, so `exactPriceReplay` is always true when a position is open. The block at `:183-230` never runs. It contains `evaluateExit`, the signal-path profit-lock update and FIX-11T's fallback Near-TP.
62:  - The comments describing that fallback (`:85-88`, `:184`, `:215-217`; `RegressionTestWorker.java:190-193`) describe behaviour that never happens.
63-- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** What actually diverges is an omission:
64-  - **Production's signal path** runs `POSITION_STOP_LOSS`, take-profit, stop-loss, the rebase hard floor and the profit lock when the signal price is authoritative, then `validateSell` (`PaperTradingService.java:113-205`).
65:  - **Replay's reachable signal path** runs only `validateSell`, and only for a bearish 1m signal (`:232-255`).
66-- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** What RPR-0.6 must add:
67-  1. **A price-assumption model, or refusal.** M06 says "Simulation records price assumptions" but names no mechanism. §7 requires "the same extracted pure policies", and Production's price policy refuses an entry without an event under 15 s old (`ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java:25, 46-48`). So `PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION` will produce zero trades wherever there are no events. The revision must choose one:
68-     - declare a fill assumption that isn't Production's (for example, candle close plus profile slippage), with its own label;
69-     - or refuse those spans, or mark them analysis-only, and report the refusal rate (O14).
70:  2. **X06 fixtures taken from Production.** The signal-path cases (take-profit, stop-loss, floor, lock, `POSITION_STOP_LOSS`) must come from `PaperTradingService`, not from Replay's dead block. RPR6-06 must register removing or rewriting that block and its comments. Removing it takes away no `wallet_settings` read, because the price path also calls `profitLockState` and `rebaseForExtendedTarget` (`:415`, `:448`).
71:  3. **Old run labels (objection 11).** `replay_price_mode` is stored on each run (`RegressionTestWorker.java:205-206`).
72-     - Existing `SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK` runs have zero trades by construction.
73:     - `EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY` is set whenever at least one event existed, even if most of the window had none.
74-  4. **Coverage per span, not one flag per run.** Together with N54, this is the coverage model the qualifier needs.
75:- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** The gaps aren't caused by purging: nothing in `src/main` deletes `market_price_event` rows. Invalid observations are never stored (`MarketPriceEventService.java:50-58`), so Production applied no protection from them either.
76-
77:**N57 (minor; extends N51/N51a). The spec defines "Replay" by name, not by component.**
78:- **[PROJECT EVIDENCE]** `com.crypto.service.TradeReplayService.replay(positionId)` loads a Production `paper_position` (`:36-37`) to build a Production trade timeline. It is an inspection view, not the regression Replay.
79:- **[AGENT INFERENCE]** §1 and §3 ban account reads "in Replay". The revision should define the boundary by package and entry point: the regression worker, shadow service, observers and `/runs/**`. Otherwise a source-scan guard (N45) will flag this class or miss Replay code elsewhere.

23-market data, persists candles and technical indicators, creates trade signals,

===== codex-docs/INVESTIGATION_LOG.md relevant =====
9-
10-- System Health CRITICAL signal-staleness alerts were confirmed as accurate for 1m and 5m analysis, not a dashboard timezone issue. At the check, candles continued through `2026-10-09T06:43:00Z` (1m) and `06:35:00Z` (5m), while the newest live signals were `04:34:03Z` (1m) and `04:30:07Z` (5m).
11-- The most recent 210-candle data-quality window has a synchronized gap for every enabled symbol: missing 1m open `2026-10-09T04:34:00Z` (KSA 07:34) and missing 5m open `2026-10-09T04:30:00Z` (KSA 07:30).
12:- Cause confirmed from the WebSocket lifecycle log: generation 1 was revoked at `2026-10-09T04:35:00.133951100Z`; generation 2 was admitted at `04:35:01.369103400Z` (1,236 ms interruption). The affected 1m and 5m candles had both closed at the reconnect boundary. Binance did not replay their final closed-kline events, leaving the already-created local rows open rather than finalized.
13-- `CandleDataQualityService` is configured with `minimum-candles: 210` and `maximum-missing-candles: 0`. Therefore one missing candle makes the entire relevant analysis window invalid. Runtime logs confirm `DATA_QUALITY_BLOCKED` with `Missing 1 candle(s) in the analysis window` for current 1m and 5m worker attempts.
14-- 1h has no comparable gap and produced a live signal at `2026-10-09T06:00:03Z` (KSA 09:00).
15-- No repair or configuration change was made. Correct remediation must restore authoritative missing candles rather than relaxing the zero-missing data-quality safety rule.
--
28-- Market path: ETH rose from 2485.88 at 07:54 KSA to 2502.77 at entry (+16.89, +0.68%). It reached 2505.56 at 10:25 KSA before the entry; entry was 2.79 (0.11%) below that high. The post-entry high was 2505.93 at 10:34 KSA, only 3.16 (0.13%) above entry, then the candle retraced. This supports the user's observation that entry occurred late in the observed move.
29-- Primary cause: the local candle-quality interruption stopped 1m ETH signals after 07:34 KSA. There were no ETH 1m signals from 07:34 to 10:26 KSA, the period containing most of the rise. The interrupted 1m and 5m rows were repaired at about 10:28 KSA, so analysis resumed only near the later consolidation/breakout attempt. This is a confirmed data-availability gap, not proof that the strategy would have bought near 07:54 KSA.
30-- Immediate signal sequence after recovery: at 10:30 KSA the 5m signal was a BUY but blocked by `RANGE_ENTRY_LOCATION`; at 10:31 KSA the 1m raw BUY was vetoed by a bearish BTC context; at 10:33 KSA BTC context became neutral and the 1m trend-following BUY was allowed. The entry signal itself explicitly recorded mixed higher-timeframe context (5m BUY, 1h NEUTRAL) and a weakening but ultra-close ask wall before its target.
31:- Assessment: the trade followed the implemented rules once local analysis resumed, and the trade was entered/exited promptly. It was nevertheless a late entry relative to the full 07:54–10:33 KSA advance because the system lacked signal coverage during the main move. The data gap is an operational defect already covered by the local-candle recovery deployment gate. Whether the unobserved earlier period would have produced an executable BUY is uncertain without a controlled Replay run; none was started because that would create replay state.

165:# Replay-versus-Production parity audit — 2026-10-10
166-
167:- Scope: source-level comparison of the manual Regression/Replay pipeline with
168-  the current paper-trading Production path. No trading code, configuration,
169:  database data, or Replay result was changed.
170:- Confirmed shared authority: Replay regenerates technical indicators through
171-  `TechnicalIndicatorService`, signals through `AnalysisService`, BUY authority
172-  through `ExecutionIntelligenceService`, BUY/SELL validation through
173-  `TradeExecutionValidationService`, and normal exit, TP-continuation,
174-  profit-lock, and near-TP policies through the corresponding shared policy
175:  classes. When canonical `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events exist, the Replay
176-  consumes them in timestamp order before making the same close-candle signal
177-  visible, matching the documented live ordering.
178:- Confirmed boundary: Replay is a shadow simulation, not a transactionally
179-  identical copy of Production. It starts with fixed capital of 10,000 USDT,
180:  manages one symbol/position in isolation, uses replay-local daily budget and
181-  position state, and writes only `*_test` tables. It therefore cannot
182-  reproduce an actual wallet balance, simultaneous positions, global
183-  `maxOpenPositions`, daily loss limit, idempotency races, database locks, or
184-  real execution latency.
185-- Confirmed historical-evidence limitation: derivatives history is not
186:  persisted, so historical Replay always receives `UNAVAILABLE` derivatives
187-  context when that feature is enabled. Historical order-book evaluation uses
188-  stored snapshots only and becomes `UNAVAILABLE` when no snapshot exists,
189-  whereas live Production samples its in-memory/live Binance source. Such runs
190:  are useful simulations but are not full signal-parity proof unless the
191-  required historical evidence exists and is reported.
192:- Confirmed price-evidence limitation: a Replay run labels itself
193:  `EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY` whenever at least one accepted price event exists. It
194-  does not prove complete tick coverage for every time an open position needed
195-  protection. Windows with no accepted events fall back to signal prices and
196-  are labelled `SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK`; neither fallback nor partial coverage
197-  should be described as tick-exact reproduction.
198-- Test evidence: Java 21 focused tests passed for profit-lock/TP-extension
199:  parity, position exit/continuation, near-TP policy, and shared sizing.
200:  Existing Replay-specific coverage is narrow: it does not execute a complete
201:  Production-versus-Replay scenario across entry, add, wallet controls,
202:  price-event ordering, partial harvest, and exit.
203:- Conclusion: Replay is suitable for controlled, manual strategy regression
204-  and the approved FIX-155 research work only when each run carries explicit
205-  evidence-quality labels. It is not yet valid to claim it is fully identical
206:  to Production in all phases. A future parity program must add deterministic
207:  scenario tests and a run-level eligibility contract before using Replay as a
208-  release-equivalence certificate.

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java lines 1-100 =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService;
   4: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   6: 
   7: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   8: import java.time.Duration;
   9: import java.time.Instant;
  10: import java.util.Optional;
  11: import java.util.Objects;
  12: 
  13: /**
  14:  * FIX-056 fresh execution-price authority.
  15:  *
  16:  * <p>TradeSignal.latestPrice is immutable decision-time evidence and must never be
  17:  * treated as an execution fill. Production resolves the newest canonical Binance 1m
  18:  * live-price event recorded by FIX-052. Administration Replay resolves the newest
  19:  * replay price event already consumed by ExecutionReplayScope. This gives both paths
  20:  * one price authority while preserving the original signal snapshot for audit.</p>
  21:  */
  22: @Service
  23: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  24: public class ExecutionPriceAuthorityService {
  25:     private static final Duration MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE = Duration.ofSeconds(15);
  26: 
  27:     private final MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService;
  28:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  29: 
  30:     public Optional<ExecutionPrice> resolve(String symbol, Instant referenceTime) {
  31:         // FIX-109 / Replay parity: wall-clock fallback is forbidden. Every caller must
  32:         // supply the evaluation clock explicitly so historical Replay can never silently
  33:         // compare an old market event with the machine clock used by Production today.
  34:         Instant reference = Objects.requireNonNull(referenceTime,
  35:                 "Execution price reference time is required");
  36: 
  37:         if (replayScope.active()) {
  38:             return replayScope.latestPrice()
  39:                     .filter(p -> symbol != null && symbol.equalsIgnoreCase(p.symbol()))
  40:                     .filter(p -> !p.observedAt().isAfter(reference))
  41:                     .filter(p -> !Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).isNegative())
  42:                     .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
  43:                     .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), "REPLAY_MARKET_PRICE_EVENT"));
  44:         }
  45: 
  46:         return marketPriceEventService.findLatestAtOrBefore(symbol, reference)
  47:                 .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
  48:                 .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), p.source()));
  49:     }
  50: 
  51:     public record ExecutionPrice(BigDecimal price, Instant observedAt, String source) {}
  52: }

  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }

  69:

   1: package com.crypto.regression.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: 
   6: import java.util.ArrayList;
   7: import java.util.List;
   8: import java.util.Objects;
   9: 
  10: /**
  11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
  12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
  16: 
  17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
  18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
  19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
  20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
  21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
  22:         }
  23: 
  24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
  25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
  26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
  27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
  28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
  29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
  30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
  31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
  32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
  33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
  34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
  35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
  36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
  37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
  38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
  42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
  43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
  44:         }
  45:     }
  46: 
  47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
  48: }

 562:     private ExitDecision evaluateExit(long runId, ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s, TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
 563:         BigDecimal price = s.getLatestPrice();
 564:         if (price == null) return ExitDecision.hold();
 565: 
 566:         // Replay uses the exact same temporal price-authority rule as production. A signal
 567:         // generated later may still carry a candle close from before entry; that historical
 568:         // price may update MTF context but may not retroactively hit TP/SL/profit-lock.
 569:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime());
 570:         if (authoritativePrice && p.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(p.takeProfit()) >= 0) {

 576:                     p.entryConfidence(), p.entryScore());

 578:                 BigDecimal distance = p.takeProfit().subtract(p.entryPrice());
 579:                 BigDecimal newTarget = p.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 580:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", p.takeProfit(), newTarget, p, continuation.explanation());
 581:                 jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, p.positionId());
 582:                 return new ExitDecision(false, "EXTEND_TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), newTarget);
 583:             }
 584:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
 585:                     runId, s.getSymbol(), p.positionId(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,

 587:                     p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.entryScore(), p.entryConfidence(), p.entryTrend(),
 588:                     p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(), oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 589:                     continuation.explanation());
 590:             persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), p, continuation.explanation());
 591:             return new ExitDecision(true, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation());
 592:         }
 593:         // FIX-052 fallback ordering also mirrors Production: STOP_LOSS is absolute and

 597:             return new ExitDecision(true, "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the stored stop loss.");

 626:             return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 627:         }
 628:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null

 651:         // Production has two SELL authorities: the live HTF PositionExitPolicy and the

 653:         // policies in the same order instead of carrying a test-only SELL rule.

 661:             if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
 662:                 return new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
 663:             }
 664:         }
 665:         return ExitDecision.hold();
 666:     }

 514:     private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
 515:                                       int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
 516:         int resolved = 0;
 517:         int size = candles.size();
 518:         for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
 519:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 520:             Candle candle = candles.get(i);
 521:             List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 522:                     symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
 523:             if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
 524:                 resolved++;
 525:             }
 526:             if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
 527:                 int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
 528:                 updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
 529:                         "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
 530:             }
 531:         }
 532:         return resolved;
 533:     }

 535:     private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
 536:             long runId,
 537:             String symbol,
 538:             Instant executionStart,
 539:             Instant executionEnd,
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
 542:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
 543:             String btcSymbol,
 544:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
 545:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,

 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
 561: 
 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;

 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 566: 
 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 592:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;

  53:     public synchronized void startRun(long runId, String symbol) {
  54:         activeByRun.remove(runId);
  55:         consumedByRun.put(runId, new java.util.HashSet<>());

  59:     public synchronized void observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
  60:             long runId, String symbol, long positionId, Instant observedAt, BigDecimal price,
  61:             Instant entryTime, BigDecimal entryPrice, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal cost,
  62:             BigDecimal stopLoss, BigDecimal takeProfit, BigDecimal highest,
  63:             boolean profitLockActive, BigDecimal profitLockPrice,
  64:             int entryScore, int entryConfidence, int entryTrend, int entryStructure,
  65:             int entryMomentum, int entryVolume,
  66:             TradeSignal current1m, TradeSignal fiveMinute, TradeSignal oneHour,
  67:             String baselineExplanation) {
  68: 
  69:         if (activeByRun.containsKey(runId) || consumedByRun.getOrDefault(runId, java.util.Set.of()).contains(positionId)) return;
  70:         if (!eligible(current1m, fiveMinute, oneHour, entryMomentum)) return;
  71: 
  72:         Instant candleIdentity = candleIdentity(current1m);
  73:         if (candleIdentity == null) return;
  74:         BigDecimal baselinePnl = price.multiply(quantity, MC).subtract(cost, MC);
  75:         BigDecimal baselinePnlPct = percentage(entryPrice, price);
  76:         BigDecimal safeHighest = highest == null ? price : highest.max(price);
  77: 
  78:         Variant v = new Variant(positionId, symbol, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, stopLoss,
  79:                 takeProfit, safeHighest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice,
  80:                 entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
  81:                 candleIdentity, current1m == null ? null : current1m.getId(), observedAt, price,
  82:                 baselinePnl, baselinePnlPct, price, price, current1m, fiveMinute, oneHour);
  83:         activeByRun.put(runId, v);
  84:         consumedByRun.computeIfAbsent(runId, k -> new java.util.HashSet<>()).add(positionId);
  85: 
  86:         jdbcTemplate.update("""

  89:                  price_at_grace,entry_price,take_profit_before_grace,take_profit_after_grace,

   1: package com.crypto.wallet.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.aopalliance.intercept.MethodInterceptor;
   4: import org.springframework.aop.framework.ProxyFactory;
   5: import org.springframework.beans.factory.config.BeanPostProcessor;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: import java.util.Set;
   8: 
   9: /** FIX-125: fail before a shared-wallet write/locking read if the caller skipped
  10:  * the global guard. This wraps Spring Data interfaces, including inherited save
  11:  * methods. It is not authorization for arbitrary raw SQL or EntityManager use;
  12:  * the named source-inventory test prohibits adding such wallet writers silently. */
  13: @Component
  14: public class WalletMutationRepositoryGuard implements BeanPostProcessor {
  15:     private static final Set<String> GUARDED=Set.of("walletAssetRepository","walletDailyStatisticsRepository",
  16:         "walletSettingsRepository","walletTradeRepository","walletCashFlowRepository","walletSnapshotRepository");
  17:     public static void check(String repository,String method) {
  18:         if(GUARDED.contains(repository) && (method.startsWith("save") || method.startsWith("delete") || method.equals("flush")
  19:             || method.contains("ForUpdate") || method.equals("creditQuantity") || method.equals("debitQuantityIfSufficient")))
  20:             WalletTransactionCoordination.requireMutation();
  21:     }
  22:     @Override public Object postProcessAfterInitialization(Object bean,String name) {
  23:         if(!GUARDED.contains(name))return bean;
  24:         ProxyFactory proxy=new ProxyFactory(bean);
  25:         proxy.addAdvice((MethodInterceptor) call->{
  26:             check(name,call.getMethod().getName());
  27:             return call.proceed();
  28:         });
  29:         return proxy.getProxy();
  30:     }
  31: }

## FINDINGS

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] Current Replay cannot substantiate 98–99% non-wallet trade-output parity. It still uses wallet settings, cash, daily budget, wallet sizing, and a run-wide price-mode flag.

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] The spec’s D01 is incorrect against the current Java: Production passes all bearish intervals to `validateSell`, but that method rejects non-1m signals. D01 should be closed as equivalent, with a fixture—not implemented as a Replay change.

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] The current fallback is effectively unreachable for an opened Replay position: without a price event, Replay cannot resolve an entry price; with one, `exactPriceReplay=true`, bypassing the fallback. Thus reachable Replay omits Production’s signal-path position management, TP/SL/profit-lock/hard-floor checks.

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] The spec’s no-account-read rule conflicts with continuing to use shared BUY evaluation: `ExecutionIntelligenceService` calls `TradeExecutionValidationService`, which reads `wallet_settings` without a Replay branch.

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] No FIX-156 qualifier, profile, certificate, per-span price coverage, forbidden-dependency exception, or earned `ANALYSIS_PARITY` implementation exists in current Java.

## EVIDENCE

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] Replay directly reads `wallet_settings`, models capital/budget, and invokes wallet sizing in [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:75), [line 318](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:318), and [line 999](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:999).

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] Shared BUY evaluation reaches account configuration: [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:653) calls `validateBuy`; that service reads `wallet_settings` at [TradeExecutionValidationService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:66) and [line 400](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:400).

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] D01’s actual behavior is 1m-only in both paths: [TradeExecutionValidationService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java:350) rejects every non-1m normal execution signal.

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] An entry needs an already-consumed fresh price event: [ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java:37). The Replay scope receives that price only from the event loop at [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:126). The fallback only runs when no events exist, at [line 183](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:183).

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] In the reachable exact-price path, Replay evaluates only a bearish 1m SELL signal at [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:232). Production first runs advisory position management and then signal-price protection checks at [PaperTradingService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:115) and [line 170](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:170).

## OBJECTIONS

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] The draft simultaneously forbids account-table reads, forbids Production changes, and keeps shared BUY validation in the Replay path. It must instead require a fully Replay-local/version-matched BUY-validation mirror, or classify all affected decisions non-comparable. Allowing the current shared account read is incompatible with the stated boundary.

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] `ANALYSIS_PARITY` explicitly disclaims lifecycle claims, while the acceptance rule requires lifecycle parity. The specification must define whether lifecycle is only a separately reported metric or introduce a compatible non-wallet lifecycle label.

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] `POSITION_STOP_LOSS` can be executed before normal Production processing, but is absent from the per-exit-class and critical-safety lists: [PositionManagementService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionManagementService.java:129).

- [AGENT INFERENCE] A raw 98–99% percentage would be misleading unless denominator coverage, minimum comparable cases, the lower-confidence-bound method, and a pre-committed final evaluation window are frozen before measurement.

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] The working tree is materially dirty, so it does not establish identity with the archived packages cited by the draft. Freeze the exact source/configuration/migration/worktree baseline before re-verifying the registers.

## UNKNOWNS

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] No database, production system, or runtime was accessed. E01–E10 measurements—especially per-span price coverage, historical configuration availability, signal availability delay, and evidence-exclusion rates—remain absent.

- [PROJECT EVIDENCE] The `StopLossEvidenceService` wrappers invoked around the otherwise 5m-only wake-up methods have not been proven side-effect-free for non-5m signals; D08 remains open pending that trace.

- [AGENT INFERENCE] It is unknown whether enough qualified, historically reconstructable decisions exist to support a statistically meaningful 98–99% lifecycle result after the required exclusions.

VERDICT: INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE

## REMAINING WORK

1. [PROJECT EVIDENCE] Revise and freeze the specification: correct D01; resolve shared BUY-validation/account-read conflict; add `POSITION_STOP_LOSS`; clarify lifecycle labeling; add the pre-entry signal-price/profit-lock suppression fixture; and close D08 wrapper effects.

2. [PROJECT EVIDENCE] Produce E01–E10 read-only evidence, including canonical per-span and final-tail price coverage, accepted-event ordering proof, immutable entry fixtures, historical configuration evidence, signal availability timing, and reconstructability/exclusion rates.

3. [PROJECT EVIDENCE] Pre-register acceptance: separate signal, BUY-decision, and lifecycle coverage/denominators; minimum counts; confidence-bound method; tolerances; final evaluation window; and zero unexplained critical-exit mismatches.

4. [PROJECT EVIDENCE] After the required approvals only, build Replay-output-table-only isolation: immutable Replay profile, local non-wallet policy mirrors, fail-closed dependency/HTTP guard, as-of qualification before output, id-preserving price evidence, per-span refusals, final-tail processing, and censored end-of-window lifecycle handling.

5. [PROJECT EVIDENCE] Replace run-wide price pass/fallback labels with decision/span evidence certificates; remove baseline observer/fallback execution; prevent `/runs/**` from reading account tables; and use staging with atomic promotion plus a bounded named Replay executor.

6. [PROJECT EVIDENCE] Validate with fixture parity, cancellation/crash/restart, async propagation, migration preflight, MySQL/Java 21 tests, resource-isolation load tests, and first-divergence reports before issuing any parity label.
